## Bog Hound — lowest CR

**Concept:** a mud-caked bog hound that drags fishermen under

| block | CR | donor | derivation | bands | structure | gates | headerBody | edition | donor | adjudicability | reader |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Bog Hound | 1 (2014) | brute-5-grappler | ok | ok | ok | ok | ok | ok | ok | ok | ok |

```
Bog Hound
Medium beast, unaligned
Armor Class

11 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

22 (3d8 + 9)

Speed: 

30 ft., swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


12 (+1)

	

8 (-1)

	

16 (+3)

	

4 (-3)

	

10 (+0)

	

4 (-3)

Skills: 

Athletics +3, Perception +2

Saving Throws: 

STR +3

Senses: 

darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 12

Languages: 

None

Challenge

1 (200 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+2

Fen Thrust Hide. When a creature hits the hound with a nonmagical weapon, the weapon lodges in the hound's body. A lodged weapon can be pulled free by a creature within reach that succeeds on a DC 13 Strength (Athletics) check as an action; while lodged, the hound can use it: its Jaws attacks deal an extra 3 (1d6) piercing damage per lodged weapon (maximum two).
Whelm. As a bonus action, the hound whelms one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While whelmed, the target is blinded and restrained, has total cover against attacks and other effects outside the hound, and takes 3 (1d6) bludgeoning damage at the start of each of the hound's turns. The hound can have only one creature whelmed at a time. If the hound takes 5 damage or more in a single turn from the whelmed creature, the hound must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the hound. If the hound dies, a whelmed creature pulls free prone by using 5 feet of movement.

Actions
Multiattack: The hound makes two Jaws attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Muck Drag.
Jaws: Melee Weapon Attack: +3 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 5 (1d8 + 1) piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 11). While grappled, the target is restrained.
Muck Drag: One creature within 20 feet of the hound must succeed on a DC 13 Strength saving throw or be pulled up to 15 feet straight toward the hound by the hound's jaws and be grappled (escape DC 11).
```

[concept] identity: A bog hound that lurks half-submerged in the muck, lunging from the reeds to seize a fisherman by the leg and dragging him down into the water before he can shout. It fights by holding on and pulling its drowning victim under, keeping its weight and jaws clamped until the struggle stops, then worrying the body into the shallows. A solitary, stubborn brute, it fights to the death over prey already gripped but slinks away from a fight it cannot win with its first strike.
[concept] lore: Born where the marsh reeds meet the fishing docks, a bog hound is the drowned mud of the fen given a hunter's hunger, and it wants only to take full nets and full stomachs down into the black water.

Languages Common

CR 24 (XP 62,000; PB +7)

Traits

Amphibious. The leviathan can breathe air and water.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the leviathan fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Actions

Multiattack. The leviathan makes three attacks, using Tentacle or Siren Bolt in any combination. It can replace one attack with a use of (A) Abyssal Whisper or (B) Spellcasting to cast Thunderwave.

Tentacle. Melee Attack Roll: +16, reach 5 ft. 36 (6d8 + 9) Thunder damage.

Siren Bolt. Ranged Attack Roll: +16, range 80/320 ft. 31 (5d8 + 9) Thunder damage.

Drowning Verse (Recharge 5-6). Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 24, each enemy in a 30-foot Emanation originating from the leviathan. Failure: 78 (12d12) Thunder damage, and the target has the Deafened condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage only.

Spellcasting. The leviathan casts one of the following spells, requiring no Material components and using Wisdom as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 24):

- At Will: Thunderwave, Fog Cloud, Hold Person, Darkness

Abyssal Whisper. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 24, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the end of the leviathan's next turn. If the target was already Frightened by this ability, it also takes 38 (11d6) Thunder damage.

Legendary Actions

Legendary Action Uses: 3. Immediately after another creature's turn, the leviathan can expend a use to take one of the following actions. It regains all expended uses at the start of each of its turns.

Crushing Coil. The leviathan makes one Tentacle attack.

Sink Away. The leviathan moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks.

Trenchborn Dread. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 24, one creature within 30 feet. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the end of its next turn.

[concept] identity: The leviathan opens a naval battle submerged, releasing a low, subsonic verse that rolls through hulls and skulls — those who hear it are deafened, confused, and frozen at the rails before a single tentacle surfaces. Each round it layers its song: a verse that compels sailors to leap overboard, a verse that shatters a ship's mast with resonant Thunder, and coils of brine-soaked hide to drag the drowning down. It toys with fleets the way a cat toys with fish, breaching only to watch the survivors scramble, and it fights to the last hull — a mythic force that has swallowed armadas and considers retreat beneath it.
[concept] lore: Born in the lightless pressure of the deepest trench, the leviathan surfaces only when the noise of a fleet offends it, and it sings until the sea is quiet again.

Pressed or cornered, it does not retreat; it drops its whole feathered-hide weight onto the threat in a crushing grapple, then drags it toward the nearest climbable surface to finish it above the ground. It toys with the wounded, circling up and around its prey to take its time.
[concept] lore: A strain of owlbear that learned that walls, trunks, and cliff ledges keep its prey close and its hide-piercing beak safe — it claims vertical territory over a hunting run and defends it against anything that strays too near the climbing routes it has blood-marked.

Reactive Heads. For each head the hydra has beyond one, it gets an extra reaction that can be used only for opportunity attacks.

Armor Class

19 (natural armor)

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the red dragon fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Actions
Multiattack: The red dragon makes three Rend attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Spellcasting to cast Scorching Ray.
Rend: Melee Weapon Attack: +14 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (2d8 + 8) slashing damage plus 5 (2d4) fire damage. If the red dragon moved at least 20 feet in a straight line toward the target immediately before the hit, the target takes an extra 13 (3d8) fire damage and, if it is Huge or smaller, is knocked prone.
Fire Breath (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 60-foot cone must make a DC 21 Dexterity saving throw, taking 59 (17d6) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Spellcasting: The red dragon casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 20, +12 to hit with spell attacks): -

At will: command, detect magic, scorching ray

1/day each: fireball
Legendary Actions

The red dragon can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The red dragon regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Commanding Presence: The red dragon uses Spellcasting to cast command (cast at 2nd level). The red dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Fiery Rays: The red dragon uses Spellcasting to cast scorching ray. The red dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Pounce: The red dragon moves up to half its speed, and it makes one Rend attack.
```

[concept] identity: An Adult Red Dragon whose left eye was punched out by a knight's lance years ago, it now hunts by sound, scent, and the heat-shimmer of bodies rather than sight — opening a fight with a strafing breath-run down the blind-side flank of whatever it has heard moving, then landing to crush and bite at close range. Every round after, it keeps its ruined eye angled toward the threat's noise, wheeling its head to feel the air, and punishes anything that tries to circle out of its good side with tail and jaws. Wounded and cornered, it does not flee — it goes into a scarred, furious death-rage, flattening terrain with breath and fighting until one side is ash.
[concept] lore: A mountain tyrant that lost an eye to a lone lancer decades ago and has never forgotten the taste of that insult, it hoards a still-growing pile of lance-torn armor from every knight since.

Buries What It Catches. When the shrike entombs a creature, the shrike buries the entombed creature in the earth beneath it; while buried, the creature is blinded and restrained and has total cover against attacks and other effects outside the shrike, and the shrike can have only one.
Trailing Scree. The ground within 10 feet of the shrike is difficult terrain.
Entomb. As a bonus action, the shrike entombs one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While entombed, the target is blinded and restrained, has total cover against attacks and other effects outside the shrike, and takes 7 (2d6) bludgeoning damage at the start of each of the shrike's turns. The shrike can have only one creature entombed at a time. If the shrike takes 15 damage or more in a single turn from the entombed creature, the shrike must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the shrike. If the shrike dies, an entombed creature breaks free prone by using 5 feet of movement.

Actions
Multiattack: The shrike makes two Claw attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Hooked Lash.
Claw: Melee Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 16 (3d8 + 3) slashing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 14). While grappled, the target is restrained.
Hooked Lash: One creature within 30 feet of the shrike must succeed on a DC 14 Strength saving throw or be pulled up to 20 feet straight toward the shrike by its grip and be grappled (escape DC 14).
```

Large undead, neutral
Armor Class

16 (natural armor)

exhaustion, poisoned

darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 16

Common plus the languages it knew in life

Challenge

10 (5,900 XP)

+4

Captain's Charm. As a bonus action, the choir targets one creature it can see within 30 feet of it. The target must succeed on a DC 15 Wisdom saving throw or be charmed by the choir until the start of the choir's next turn.

Actions
Multiattack: The choir makes three attacks, using Tide Lash or Gravewater Ribbon. It can replace one attack with a use of Drowning Note.
Tide Lash: Melee Spell Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 12 (2d8 + 3) bludgeoning damage plus 7 (2d6) necrotic damage.
Gravewater Ribbon: Ranged Spell Attack: +7 to hit, range 60 ft., one target. Hit: 16 (3d8 + 3) necrotic damage.
Drowning Note: One creature within 60 feet of the choir must succeed on a DC 15 Constitution saving throw or take 18 (4d8) necrotic damage and be deafened until the end of its next turn.
Spellcasting: The choir casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 15):

At will: minor illusion, mage hand
1/day each: fear, slow
Hymn of the Drowned: One creature within 60 feet of the choir must succeed on a DC 15 Wisdom saving throw or be frightened of the choir until the end of the choir's next turn. If the target was already frightened by this ability, it also takes 21 (6d6) necrotic damage.

Searing Brass Hide. A creature that touches the tyrant or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 10 (3d6) Fire damage.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the tyrant fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Immutable Form. The tyrant can't shape-shift.

Siege Monster. The tyrant deals double damage to objects and structures.

Actions

Multiattack. The tyrant makes two Molten Fist attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Molten Lash.

Molten Fist. Melee Attack Roll: +12, reach 10 ft. 33 (6d8 + 6) Fire damage.

Forge Breath (Recharge 5-6). Strength Saving Throw: DC 19, each creature in a 30-foot Cone. Failure: 72 (16d8) Fire damage, and the target has the Prone condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage only.

Molten Lash. Strength Saving Throw: DC 19, one creature within 30 feet that is Prone. Failure: 28 (8d6) Fire damage. Success: Half damage.

Legendary Actions

Legendary Action Uses: 3. Immediately after another creature's turn, the tyrant can expend a use to take one of the following actions. It regains all expended uses at the start of each of its turns.

Iron Advance. The tyrant moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks, and it makes one Molten Fist attack.

Forge-City Advance. The tyrant moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks. During this movement, it can move through Large or smaller objects and structures, destroying them.

Quake Strike. Strength Saving Throw: DC 19, each creature within 10 feet of the tyrant. Failure: The target has the Prone condition.

Fearsome Bellow: One creature within 60 feet of the exile must succeed on a DC 15 Wisdom saving throw or be frightened of the exile until the end of the exile's next turn. If the target was already frightened by this ability, it also takes 27 (6d8) thunder damage.

Krennik Skullstandard
Medium humanoid, neutral evil
Armor Class

14 (leather)

Hit Points: 

78 (12d8 + 24)

50 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA

passive Perception 13

+2

Mounted Agility (Bonus Action). Krennik takes the Dash or Disengage action.
Standard's Rush (1/Day) (Bonus Action). Immediately after dealing damage to a creature that is already below half its hit point maximum, Krennik moves up to half its Speed, and it makes one Saber attack.
Riposte (Reaction). Trigger: A creature within 5 feet of Krennik misses it with a melee attack roll. Response: Krennik makes one Saber attack against that creature.

Actions
Multiattack: Krennik makes two attacks, using Saber or Javelin.
Saber: Melee Weapon Attack: +5 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 10 (2d6 + 3) slashing damage. If Krennik moved at least 20 feet in a straight line toward the target immediately before the hit, the target takes an extra 4 (1d8) slashing damage and, if it is Medium or smaller, is knocked prone.
Javelin: Ranged Weapon Attack: +5 to hit, range 30/120 ft., one target. Hit: 7 (1d8 + 3) piercing damage.
Skullcharge (Recharge 5-6): Krennik moves up to its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks and can move through the spaces of Medium or smaller creatures. Each creature whose space it enters must make a DC 13 Strength saving throw, taking 22 (5d8) bludgeoning damage on a failed save. On a failed save, the target is knocked prone.

Chillgrip Lattice

Small Construct, Unaligned

AC 14

Resistances Cold

Immunities Poison; Charmed, Exhaustion, Frightened, Paralyzed, Petrified, Poisoned

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 12

Languages Understands the languages of its creator but can't speak

CR 4 (XP 1,100; PB +2)

Traits

Shallow-Channel Herding. While the lattice is in a shallow channel or similar sure footing, the lattice herds creatures toward the channels, and while it keeps its footing there it has Advantage on checks and saves to Grapple or avoid being moved.

Chilling Body. A creature that touches the lattice or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 5 (1d10) Cold damage.

Actions

Multiattack. The lattice makes two attacks, using Ironshod Clamp or Seizing Lunge in any combination. It can replace one attack with a use of Channeling Hook.

Ironshod Clamp. Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. 8 (1d8 + 4) Bludgeoning damage plus 6 (1d12) Cold damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 14). While Grappled, the target has the Restrained condition.

Channeling Hook. Strength Saving Throw: DC 13, one creature within 30 feet. Failure: The target is pulled up to 20 feet straight toward the lattice by its grip and has the Grappled condition (escape DC 14).

Seizing Lunge. Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. 8 (1d8 + 4) Bludgeoning damage. If the target is a Medium or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 14).

Bonus Actions

Cage. The lattice cages one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While caged, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the lattice, and takes 6 (1d12) Cold damage at the start of each of the lattice's turns. The lattice can have only one creature caged at a time. If the lattice takes 12 damage or more in a single turn from the caged creature, the lattice must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the lattice. If the lattice dies, a caged creature pulls free Prone by using 5 feet of movement.

[concept] synthesis text: A four-legged lattice of moss-choked bog-iron, the Drowned Prowler is a construct of woven reeds and rusted chains that the swamp itself grew over, its hollow chest packed with frost-mud that exhales pale mist with every stride. It stalks intruders patiently along its own boundary, herding them into the shallow channels where its footing is sure, then lunges to seize and pin a target beneath its freezing weight, its cold-soaked limbs numbing whatever they clamp. It never chases beyond its marked ground; wounded or not, it fights to the last of its hoarded territory, and its small, cunning intelligence spends every round repositioning to drive prey back toward the deep water it defends. It is scheming (INT 14-16), and it is a territorial guardian that defends its home ground to the death.

Vampire
Medium undead, lawful evil
Armor Class

16 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

195 (23d8 + 92)

40 ft., climb 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

18 (+4)

	

18 (+4)

	

17 (+3)

	

15 (+2)

	

18 (+4)

Skills: 

Perception +7, Stealth +14

Saving Throws: 

DEX +9, INT +8

Damage Resistances: 

necrotic

Damage Immunities: 

poison

Condition Immunities: 

exhaustion, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 17

Languages: 

Common plus two other languages

Challenge

13 (10,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+5

Legendary Resistance. If the vampire fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Misty Escape. If the vampire drops to 0 hit points outside its resting place, the vampire uses Shape-Shift to become mist (no action required). If it can't use Shape-Shift, it is destroyed.
While it has 0 hit points in mist form, it can't return to its vampire form, and it must reach its resting place within 2 hours or be destroyed. Once in its resting place, it returns to its vampire form and is paralyzed until it regains any hit points, and it regains 1 hit point after spending 1 hour there.
Spider Climb. The vampire can climb difficult surfaces, including along ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.
Vampire Weakness. The vampire has these weaknesses: Forbiddance: the vampire can't enter a residence without an invitation from an occupant.
Running Water: the vampire takes 20 acid damage if it ends its turn in running water.
Stake to the Heart: If a weapon that deals piercing damage is driven into the vampire's heart while the vampire is incapacitated in its resting place, the vampire is paralyzed until the weapon is removed.
Sunlight: the vampire takes 20 radiant damage if it starts its turn in sunlight. While in sunlight, it has disadvantage on attack rolls and ability checks.
Shape-Shift. As a bonus action, if the vampire isn't in sunlight or running water, it shape-shifts into a Tiny bat (Speed 5 ft., Fly Speed 30 ft.) or a Medium cloud of mist (Speed 5 ft., Fly Speed 20 ft. [hover]), or it returns to its vampire form. Anything it is wearing transforms with it.
While in bat form, the vampire can't speak. Its game statistics, other than its size and Speed, are unchanged.
While in mist form, the vampire can't take any actions, speak, or manipulate objects. It is weightless and can enter an enemy's space and stop there. If air can pass through a space, the mist can do so, but it can't pass through liquid. It has resistance to all damage, except the damage it takes from sunlight.
Charm (Recharge 5-6). As a bonus action, the vampire casts charm person, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17), and the duration is 24 hours. The charmed target is a willing recipient of the vampire's Bite, the damage of which doesn't end the spell. When the spell ends, the target is unaware it was charmed by the vampire.

Actions
Multiattack: The vampire makes three Grave Strike attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Bite (Bat or Vampire Form Only).
Grave Strike: Melee Weapon Attack: +9 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (3d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage plus 7 (2d6) necrotic damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 17) by one of the vampire's two hands.
Bite (Bat or Vampire Form Only): One creature within 5 feet of the vampire that is willing or is grappled, incapacitated, or restrained must make a DC 17 Constitution saving throw, taking 6 (1d4 + 4) piercing damage plus 13 (3d8) necrotic damage on a failed save. The target's hit point maximum decreases by an amount equal to the necrotic damage taken, and the vampire regains hit points equal to that amount. A humanoid reduced to 0 hit points by this damage and then buried rises the following sunset as a vampire spawn under the vampire's control.
```

[concept] identity: The Thorn-Gardener opens by vanishing into its own briar hedge, letting the blood-fed thorns mask its approach, then erupts from the wall of roses onto a single victim with a lunging, exsanguinating first strike. Every round after, it withdraws into the thorn-channel it has grown through the garden, pruning prey with whip-crack lashes of iron-barbed vine while it drinks a little from each wound to knit its own flesh shut. It toys with intruders with a gardener's patience, only abandoning its cultivated ground when the fight turns truly against it.

audit finding below confidence gate: Aura of Authority The aura grants its benefit to 'the archon and its allies' within the emanation, but the archon is by definition the origin of its own emanation, so naming the archon separately is redundant; more substantively the 'allies' benefit is unresolvable for a solo monster block with no ally creation — this is a parked ally-reference, not flagged.

Traits

Tunneler. The worm can burrow through solid rock at half its Burrow Speed and leaves a 10-foot-diameter tunnel in its wake.

Actions

Multiattack. The worm makes one Bite attack and one Tail Stinger attack.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 47 (11d6 + 9) Piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 22), and it has the Restrained condition until the grapple ends.

Tail Stinger. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 30 (6d6 + 9) Piercing damage plus 35 (10d6) Poison damage.

Bonus Actions

Swallow. Strength Saving Throw: DC 21, one Large or smaller creature Grappled by the worm (it can have up to three creatures swallowed at a time). Failure: The target is swallowed by the worm, and the Grappled condition ends. A swallowed creature has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the worm, and takes 17 (5d6) Acid damage at the start of each of the worm's turns.
If the worm takes 30 damage or more on a single turn from a creature inside it, the worm must succeed on a DC 21 Constitution saving throw at the end of that turn or regurgitate all swallowed creatures, each of which falls in a space within 5 feet of the worm and has the Prone condition. If the worm dies, any swallowed creature no longer has the Restrained condition and can escape from the corpse using 20 feet of movement, exiting Prone.

Skills Athletics +14, Perception +10

Resistances Cold

Immunities Fire, Poison; Poisoned

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 20

Languages Infernal; telepathy 120 ft.

CR 20 (XP 25,000; PB +6)

Traits

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the fiend fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Diabolical Restoration. If the fiend dies outside the Nine Hells, its body disappears in sulfurous smoke, and it gains a new body instantly, reviving with all its Hit Points somewhere in the Nine Hells.

Fear Aura. The fiend emanates an aura in a 20-foot Emanation while it doesn't have the Incapacitated condition. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 21, any enemy that starts its turn in the aura. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this pit fiend's aura for 24 hours.

Magic Resistance. The fiend has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

Actions

Multiattack. The fiend makes one Bite attack, two Devilish Claw attacks, and one Fiery Mace attack.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 30 (5d8 + 8) Piercing damage. If the target is a creature, it must make the following saving throw. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 21. Failure: The target has the Poisoned condition. While Poisoned, the target can't regain Hit Points and takes 21 (6d6) Poison damage at the start of each of its turns, and it repeats the save at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. After 1 minute, it succeeds automatically.

Fiery Mace. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 17 (2d8 + 8) Force damage plus 21 (6d6) Fire damage.

Devilish Claw. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 17 (2d8 + 8) Necrotic damage.

Hellfire Spellcasting (Recharge 4-6). The fiend casts Fireball (level 5 version) twice, requiring no Material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 21). It can replace one Fireball with Hold Monster (level 7 version) or Wall of Fire.

Reactions

Riposte. Trigger: A creature within 5 feet of the fiend misses it with a melee attack roll. Response: The fiend makes one Bite attack against that creature.

22 (natural armor)

fire

blindsight 60 ft., darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 20

Common, Draconic

24 (62,000 XP)

+7

Legendary Resistance (4/Day). If the gold dragon fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Amphibious. The gold dragon can breathe air and water.

Actions
Multiattack: The gold dragon makes three Rend attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of (A) Spellcasting to cast Guiding Bolt (level 4 version) or (B) Weakening Breath.
Rend: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 15 ft., one target. Hit: 19 (2d8 + 10) slashing damage plus 9 (2d8) fire damage.
Spellcasting: The gold dragon casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 24, +16 to hit with spell attacks):

At will: detect magic, guiding bolt, shapechange
1/day each: flame strike, word of recall, zone of truth
Fire Breath (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 90-foot cone must make a DC 24 Dexterity saving throw, taking 71 (13d10) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Weakening Breath: Each creature that isn't currently affected by this breath in a 90-foot cone must make a DC 24 Strength saving throw. On a failed save, the target has disadvantage on Strength-based d20 tests and subtracts 5 (1d10) from its damage rolls. It repeats the save at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. After 1 minute, it succeeds automatically.
Legendary Actions

The gold dragon can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The gold dragon regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Banish: One creature the gold dragon can see within 120 feet of it must make a DC 24 Charisma saving throw. On a failed save, the target takes 24 (7d6) force damage, and the target is incapacitated and transported to a harmless demiplane until the start of the gold dragon's next turn, at which point it reappears in an unoccupied space of the gold dragon's choice within 120 feet of the gold dragon. Whether it succeeds or fails, the gold dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Guiding Light: The gold dragon uses Spellcasting to cast guiding bolt (cast at 4th level).
Pounce: The gold dragon moves up to half its speed, and it makes one Rend attack.
```

Traits

Caustic Shell. A creature that touches the plaguewretch or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 7 (2d6) Acid damage.

Actions

Multiattack. The plaguewretch makes two attacks, using Scythe Arm or Carving Scythe in any combination.

Scythe Arm. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 10 ft. 18 (4d6 + 4) Slashing damage plus 13 (3d8) Acid damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 16). While Grappled, the target has the Restrained condition.

Caustic Spray (Recharge 5-6). Constitution Saving Throw: DC 15, each creature in a 30-foot Cone. Failure: 32 (5d12) Acid damage, and the target has the Poisoned condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage only.

Carving Scythe. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 5 ft. 18 (4d6 + 4) Slashing damage.

Bonus Actions

Cage. The plaguewretch cages one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While caged, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the plaguewretch, and takes 10 (3d6) Acid damage at the start of each of the plaguewretch's turns. The plaguewretch can have only one creature caged at a time. If the plaguewretch takes 25 damage or more on a single turn from the caged creature, the plaguewretch must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 10 feet of the plaguewretch. If the plaguewretch dies, a caged creature pulls free Prone by using 10 feet of movement.

Blackiron Sentinel
Huge construct, lawful neutral
Armor Class

20 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

168 (16d12 + 64)

20 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

8 (-1)

	

18 (+4)

	

7 (-2)

CON +8

poison

charmed, exhaustion, frightened, paralyzed, petrified, poisoned

darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 14

Languages: 

understands the languages of its creator but can't speak

Challenge

11 (7,200 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+4

Blackiron Rebuke. A creature that touches the sentinel or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 10 (3d6) bludgeoning damage.
Ward Bond. The sentinel is magically bound to a ward of its choosing, and it can have only one ward at a time. While the sentinel is within 60 feet of its ward, half of any damage the ward takes (round up) is transferred to the sentinel.
Regeneration. The sentinel regains 10 hit points at the start of each of its turns if it has at least 1 hit point.
Ironback Reprisal. As a bonus action, the sentinel deals 10 (3d6) bludgeoning damage to one creature within 5 feet that hit it with an attack roll since the end of the sentinel's last turn.
Interpose (Reaction). When a creature within 5 feet of the sentinel that it guards is hit by an attack roll, the attack hits the sentinel instead, and the sentinel has resistance to the attack's damage.

Actions
Multiattack: The sentinel makes two Iron Fist attacks.
Iron Fist: Melee Weapon Attack: +8 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 25 (6d6 + 4) bludgeoning damage.
Oathbound (2/Day): The sentinel targets itself or one creature it can see within 60 feet. The target gains 24 (7d6) temporary hit points, and it gains a +2 bonus to AC until the end of the sentinel's next turn.

Trespasser's Curse. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 15, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the end of its next turn.

Bonus Actions

False Form. The drowned shape-shifts to resemble an ordinary object or fixture of the place it waits in while retaining its game statistics, or it returns to its true form. While it is motionless in its false form, it is indistinguishable from that feature.

DEX +7, WIS +5

Senses: 

passive Perception 15

Languages: 

Common, Draconic

Challenge

8 (3,900 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+3

Wounded Frenzy. While Meshrin has half of its hit points or fewer, it has advantage on attack rolls and saving throws.
Hold Breath. Meshrin can hold its breath for 15 minutes.
Great Weapon Master. When Meshrin hits a creature with its greataxe, the attack deals an extra 3 damage.
Marsh Stride. Difficult terrain composed of mud doesn't cost Meshrin extra movement, and Meshrin can move across mud without needing to make an ability check.
Fleet Agility. As a bonus action, Meshrin takes the Dash or Disengage action.

Actions
Multiattack: Meshrin makes three attacks, using Greataxe or Javelin.
Greataxe: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (3d8 + 4) slashing damage.
Javelin: Ranged Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, range 30/120 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (3d8 + 4) piercing damage. Immediately after the attack, a javelin returns to Meshrin's grasp.
Frightening Roar (Recharge 5-6): Each creature within 30 feet of Meshrin must succeed on a DC 15 Wisdom saving throw or be frightened of Meshrin until the end of its next turn.

[concept] identity: Meshrin opens by hurling a javelin from 30 feet out, then closes the gap with a ground-eating charge to bring his greataxe down two-handed. Once locked in melee he rages, shrugging off pain and answering every wound with a heavier, wilder swing — and when prey breaks and runs, he lets loose a terrifying roar to pin it in place before the axe falls. He fights to the death; a cornered Meshrin does not retreat, he rears up and makes the last swing count.
[concept] lore: A scarred lizardfolk berserker who was driven from his spawning marsh for eating a war-chief's champion, Meshrin now sells his axe to any warband that will keep him fed and let him loose on their enemies.
